## 10. Important evidence limitations
- Git history cannot prove every GitHub UI operation.
- Deleted remote refs, force-push events and server audit logs may not be recoverable from this clone.
- Local reflog is local evidence and is not uploaded as a GitHub reflog.
- Commit dates can be authored or configured by clients; they are not independent proof of wall-clock activity.
- No claim of complete server-side audit coverage is made.

## 11. Secret-scanning and redaction policy
- No PAT, OAuth token, password, private key, cookie, authorization header, or secret value is permitted in this report.
- No secret-scanning bypass or unblock URL is to be used.
- If a credential is detected, redact it and remove it from the commit being pushed.

## 12. Audit status
Generated from available Git objects and local evidence. Review limitations above before treating this as a complete server-side audit.
